<script type="text/javascript">
//<!--
/* <![CDATA[ */
	jQuery(document).bind("page:ready", function(event) {

		var context = PageContext.currentDataModel;
		var delay = 1500;
		var countryCookieName = 'cs_selectedcountry';
		var hasSelectedCoutryCode = 'NONE';
		var deleteCountryCookie = true; 
		
		
		// Search for Country Cookie
		try {
			if(typeof CookieUtils.readJSONCookie(countryCookieName) != 'undefined') {
				hasSelectedCoutryCode = CookieUtils.readJSONCookie(countryCookieName);
			};
						
		} catch(ignore) { };	

	
		// Show Counten layer for soecial Dynamic Groups and optionaly delete country Cookie
		function showChangecountryLayer() {
		
			// Get geotargeting Dialog Contetn Asset ID over Cusotmer Group
			var openGeotargetingLayerContent = 'NONE';
			var openGeotargetingLayerTitle = 'Countrydetection Advice';
			
			try {
				jQuery.each(context.customergroups, function() {

					switch (this.id) {
						case 'GeoregionGuestCZ':
							openGeotargetingLayerContent = 'GEOREGIOINFO_CONTENTLAYER_CZ';
							openGeotargetingLayerTitle = 'GEOREGIOINFO_CONTENTLAYER_CZ Title';
						
							break;

					};
								
				});
								
			} catch(ignore) {  };	
		

	
			var options = {
				width       : 400,
				height      : 'auto',
				iFrameUse   : false,
				dialogClass : 'changecountryLayer',
				draggable   : true,
				position    : 'center'
			};
			
			// Open layer if ther is an dynamic Customer group Detected and already an countryCookie is detected
			if(openGeotargetingLayerContent != 'NONE' && hasSelectedCoutryCode != 'NONE' ) {
				ContentUtils.loadAssetLayer(openGeotargetingLayerContent, openGeotargetingLayerTitle, options);
				
				// Optinal: delete axisting Coountry Cookie
				if (deleteCountryCookie) {
					CookieUtils.deleteCookie(countryCookieName);
				}
				
			};
			
		
		};
		
		// run Function with small timeout
		setTimeout(showChangecountryLayer, delay);
	
	});
	
	/* ]]> */
// -->
</script>

<div class="privacylayer__content">
<div class="privacylayer__headline">
Your privacy settings
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__text">
In order to be able to provide you with an optimal user experience in our online shop and with our advertisements, an experience tailored to your unique needs, we work with various service providers and technology providers, using cookies and tracking methods in the process. We have summarized all of these services to provide you with the following overview. Select for yourself what you would like to consent to with regards to data processing according to Article 6(1) lit. a EU GDPR. You can withdraw your consent at any time by changing the privacy settings. You will find further information about the individual service providers and data collection in our <a class="privacylayer__link" href="https://www.soliver.eu/general_privacy-GENERAL_PRIVACY.html">Privacy Policies</a>.
</div>
<form action="" class="jsPrivacyLayerForm privacylayer__form">
<div class="privacylayer__channel">
<div class="privacylayer__channelheadline">
<div class="privacylayer__channeltoggle">
<label class="privacylayer__togglelabel">
<input class="jsPrivacyLayerCheckbox privacylayer__togglecheckbox" name="marketing_searchengines" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="privacylayer__toggleswitch"></span>
</label>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channelname">Search engines</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channeldescription">
We use tracking methods from search engine providers to optimise our advertisements so that you can easily find our online shop in the standard search engines, even with relevant future search queries. For example, to determine the relevance of an advertisement, we record the categories that are interesting to the shop’s visitor, and whether an order was placed. These data are pseudonymised and transferred to the search engine providers.
</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channel">
<div class="privacylayer__channelheadline">
<div class="privacylayer__channeltoggle">
<label class="privacylayer__togglelabel">
<input class="jsPrivacyLayerCheckbox privacylayer__togglecheckbox" name="marketing_display" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="privacylayer__toggleswitch"></span>
</label>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channelname">Display</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channeldescription">
In order to be able to show you relevant advertising banners on external websites, we work together with display networks and service providers. To optimize advertisements, we use cookies and tracking methods that collect data about surfing behaviour, save that data in cookies and transfer the data, pseudonymised, to the technology providers.
</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channel">
<div class="privacylayer__channelheadline">
<div class="privacylayer__channeltoggle">
<label class="privacylayer__togglelabel">
<input class="jsPrivacyLayerCheckbox privacylayer__togglecheckbox" name="marketing_portals" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="privacylayer__toggleswitch"></span>
</label>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channelname">Portals</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channeldescription">
In addition to our online shop, we also present our most exciting offers and most popular products on external product profiles so that they are easier to find. To optimise product selection, we use cookies and tracking methods, for example, in order to determine which items are particularly popular and if an order is placed as a result of an advertisement.
</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channel">
<div class="privacylayer__channelheadline">
<div class="privacylayer__channeltoggle">
<label class="privacylayer__togglelabel">
<input class="jsPrivacyLayerCheckbox privacylayer__togglecheckbox" name="marketing_affiliate" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="privacylayer__toggleswitch"></span>
</label>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channelname">Affiliates</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channeldescription">
We work together with affiliate programmes and use their cookies and tracking methods so that you do not miss any exciting promotions and can benefit from bonus programmes. Affiliate programmes are the partner networks of individual website operators such as voucher portals, bonus programmes and bloggers. These technologies help us to determine if the advertisements introduced by the affiliates have generated an order. This benefits both you and the partners.
</div>
</div>

</label>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channelname">Retargeting</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channeldescription">
We work together with retargeting providers so that we can show you advertising banners on external websites with relevant products from our online shop which you may have been interested in, but which you have not yet purchased. In order to do so, transaction data from our website are recorded, e.g., items you have viewed or placed in your shopping cart. Please do not be surprised: If you decide against the collection of data for the purpose of retargeting, a new window will open which will take you to the website of our retargeting provider. In order to ensure that data will no longer be forwarded to the partners, please follow the instructions on their website and then save your details to conclude the process.
</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channel">
<div class="privacylayer__channelheadline">
<div class="privacylayer__channeltoggle">
<label class="privacylayer__togglelabel">
<input class="jsPrivacyLayerCheckbox privacylayer__togglecheckbox" name="marketing_social" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="privacylayer__toggleswitch"></span>
</label>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channelname">Social networks</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channeldescription">
We use the cookies and tracking technologies of social media channels to show you exciting promotions and customised advertisements on your preferred social networks. In order to do so, pseudonymised data are transferred to the networks and merged, if necessary, with additional data by the network.
</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channel">
<div class="privacylayer__channelheadline">
<div class="privacylayer__channeltoggle">
<label class="privacylayer__togglelabel">
<input class="jsPrivacyLayerCheckbox privacylayer__togglecheckbox" name="marketing_personalisation" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="privacylayer__toggleswitch"></span>
</label>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channelname">Personalisation</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__channeldescription">
Your individualised shopping experience is very important to us! We collect data from your movement and purchasing behaviour in our shop so that we can provide you with customised product recommendations in the online shop and in the newsletter. We use cookies and tracking methods in order to do so.
</div>
</div>
<div class="privacylayer__buttonwrap">
<div class="jsPrivacyLayerSubmit privacylayer__button">Save</div>
</div>
</form>
</div>
</div>
</div>
